src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:1:{-# OPTIONS --lossy-unification -WnoUnsupportedIndexedMatch #-}
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:6:   a concatenation `A ⊗ B` has at most one splitting -- the reason it is the
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:57:-- transport along maps, in either argument
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:66:-- ...and through each connective on the right
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:95:-- ε is sequentially unambiguous with everything, on either side: it has no
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:96:-- last character to follow and no first character to begin with.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:98:-- `c ∉FollowLast εTy` unfolds to `¬Nullable (εTy ⊗ startsWith c)`
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:104:-- If `c` cannot follow `A`, then `A` is sequentially unambiguous with
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:105:-- anything beginning with `c`: any other character cannot begin it.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:114:    -- two different characters cannot both head the same word
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:126:-- The right factor's first character, made explicit.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:132:-- ...and `⊤Ty` on the right of a `startsWith` is absorbed.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:136:-- The point of `⊛`: if `A` is sequentially unambiguous with a non-nullable
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:137:-- `B`, then an `A`-word cannot also be an `A` followed by a `B` followed by
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:138:-- anything.  Whichever side the separation refuses `B`'s first character
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:139:-- to, that side gives the contradiction.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:157:-- `⊕` on the left of a `&`, the mirror of `⊕-elim&`.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:164:-- The keystone.  Two ways of reading one word as `A` followed by something
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:165:-- must cut it in the same place, because the letter at which the two cuts
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:166:-- differ would have to both follow `A` and open the right factor.  This is
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:167:-- `Precise.⊗&-align`; the old proof went through the external splitting
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:168:-- trichotomy and two four-fold `⊕ᴰ-elim`s.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:178:-- ...and at three factors, which is the shape the star argument wants.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:185:-- Closure of `∉FollowLast` under the connectives.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:187:-- With `B` non-nullable the `c` is `B`'s business alone: the separation
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:188:-- pins the cut, and `c ∉FollowLast B` finishes.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:197:-- If `B` may be empty, split on whether it is.  Empty: the `c` follows `A`
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:198:-- directly, so `c ∉First B` refutes it after `∉FollowLast→⊛` pins the cut.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:199:-- Nonempty: replace `B` by `B & char⁺` and split again on the *other*
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:200:-- copy of `B` -- empty there is `⊛→must-split`, nonempty is the previous
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:201:-- lemma.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:213:    -- `B` empty: the `c` is the one that follows `A`
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:221:    -- ...and the other copy of `B` is empty, so the whole `c`-suffix sits
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:222:    -- past a complete `A`
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:250:-- A `c` after an `A ⊕ B` either follows the summand it started, or crosses
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:251:-- from one to the other.  The crossing is refuted by `#`: the two summands
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:252:-- cannot share a first letter, and whichever of them cannot be empty
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:253:-- supplies the character to clash on.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:302:-- Brüggemann-Klein and Wood's star theorem.  The `c` may sit arbitrarily
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:303:-- deep, so the refutation is a fold with carrier `¬Ty (FollowLastTy (A *) c)
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:304:-- & (A *)`; each cons step pins the two cuts with `⊗&-distL` twice.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:313:    -- a nonempty `A *` is an `A` followed by an `A *`
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:342:      -- the prefix before the `c` is empty, so `c` opens the whole `A *`
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:347:      -- ...the trailing `A *` is empty, so the `c` has nowhere to sit
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:354:      -- ...and the real case: both cuts are pinned and the tail's own
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:355:      -- refutation applies
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:366:      -- the prefix is nonempty, so it is `A ⊗ A *` and the trailing `A *`
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:367:      -- decides which of the two above applies
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:401:-- Unambiguity of the connectives, over the same separations.  `⊗` is the
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:402:-- one with content: `splitAgree` makes the two cuts coincide, and then the
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/SequentialUnambiguity/Base.agda:403:-- factors agree by their own unambiguity.

src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:1:-- Parser combinators inspired by agdarsec (Allais)

src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:3:{-# OPTIONS --lossy-unification -WnoUnsupportedIndexedMatch #-}
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:7:   in `Ans`.  What the combinators ask of it is not a monad -- `bind` is
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:8:   never used -- but `AnswerFunctor`: an action on *isomorphisms*, a
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:13:   `CovariantAnswer`, not here -- one cannot refuse to decide.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:24:   above it -- the table, the cover, `Choice`, the fixpoint -- mentions the
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:46:-- the grammar `Maybe` of `Types` shadows this one, so it stays qualified
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:67:-- Splitting on the lookahead class of the current string.  Independent of
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:68:-- what an answer is; the three token rules are all built from it.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:73:-- Coarsening a cover along a routing of its cells.  Nothing here mentions an
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:74:-- answer: it is the table -- a plain `I → Maybe Y` -- and the cover it
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:75:-- induces.  Lives here rather than in `Decidable/Routed` because every
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:76:-- answer routes; only the *commitment* differs.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:103:-- The `isSet` a routed sum is indexed by.  A definition rather than an
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:104:-- inlined `Discrete→isSet` so that a grammar may name the *same* proof the
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:105:-- `Choice` module will use: `⊕ᴰSet` is definitional in its proof.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:121:-- ...and the one-token cover is the instance the LL(1) parsers use.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:125:-- The five isomorphisms the combinators transport along.  `Unitor` and
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:126:-- `Types` prove the round trips; these only bundle them.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:144:-- Isomorphisms of grammars compose and invert.  The wildcat has these, but
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:145:-- the grammar `≅` is a private module application there, so they are
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:146:-- re-bundled at the concrete type.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:161:-- A `⊢`-term into a `TheorySet` is a set, so an isomorphism is determined by
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:162:-- its two maps: the round-trip fields are propositions.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:188:  -- `Lift` has η, so both round trips are `refl`.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:195:  -- the unit on the right, at the lifted `ε` a continuation carries
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:209:    -- the action on isomorphisms.  `Dec` needs the backward map to move a
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:210:    -- refutation and `Maybe`/`ND` ignore it, but no combinator here ever
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:211:    -- has two independent maps to give: it always has one isomorphism.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:226:-- A covariant answer additionally has a plain `fmap` and an empty answer.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:227:-- `Dec` has neither: transporting a refutation needs the backward map, and
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:228:-- `⊤Ty ⊢ DecTy A` at an arbitrary `A` is a decision procedure, not a
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:229:-- default.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:237:-- A divariant answer transports along a pair of maps that need not be
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:238:-- inverse.  Nothing in `Core` or `Syntax` needs it -- the grammars' own
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:239:-- `roll`/`unroll` pairs are proved to be isomorphisms, so they go through
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:240:-- `mapP≅`.  It exists because `Dec`'s client-facing `mapP` is genuinely
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:241:-- divariant: a caller may transport a decision along a retract.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:248:-- Committing on a route.  `_<|>_` consults every alternative; a *route*
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:249:-- names one cell of a cover and answers from the branch that cell selects.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:250:--
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:251:-- This is the one place where the three answers are not interchangeable.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:252:-- At `Dec` the field is `routeIn`: the branch may come back `no`, and the
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:253:-- cover's `disjoint` is what turns that refutation into a refutation of the
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:254:-- whole sum.  A covariant answer has no refutation to turn, but it does not
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:255:-- need one -- `Ans-empty` answers the `nothing` cell and a failed branch
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:256:-- fails the sum -- so `FromCov.committing` derives it.  What is *not* derivable
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:257:-- from `AnswerFunctor` alone is either half: an answer must say how it
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:258:-- disposes of the cells it did not take.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:267:-- `AnswerFunctor` constrains types, not behaviour: an instance is free to
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:268:-- define `Ans-≅ φ = <discard the answer>` and still typecheck.  These two
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:269:-- laws say `Ans` really is a functor on grammars-and-isomorphisms, and they
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Core.agda:270:-- are what any theorem *about* the combinators has to start from.  Kept

src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:1:{-# OPTIONS --lossy-unification -WnoUnsupportedIndexedMatch #-}
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:7:   which forces the end state, and extending stays O(1) -- it is `STEP`.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:71:  -- Traces with both endpoints named.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:73:  -- the `Tag` of `Deterministic`, with the acceptance equation replaced
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:74:  -- by an equation on the *end state*: stopping is allowed only there.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:84:  -- `TraceTo q q'`: a run from `q` that *ends* at `q'`.  The end state is
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:85:  -- the recursion's parameter, the start state its index.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:115:  -- The bridge to `Trace`.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:117:  -- forget the end state, remembering only that it accepts
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:132:  -- ...and recover it.  `Trace b q` is `⊕[ q' ] (b ≡ isAcc q') × TraceTo q q'`
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:149:  -- the two together.  `bridge` is the forward map of `Trace≅TraceTo`
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:150:  -- below; its section is free from the unambiguity of `Trace`.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:162:  -- The end state is a function of the word: `δ*`.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:177:  -- ...so the `⊕[ q' ]` of `BridgeTy` is a *singleton* sum
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:186:  -- Cancellation: `δ` really is followed.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:187:  --
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:188:  -- If `u` takes `q` to `q'` and `u ++ z` is a run of `q`, then `z` is a
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:189:  -- run of `q'`.  Induction on the `TraceTo`; the `Trace` is inverted at
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:190:  -- each step by the precision of `literal` (`flatEq` + cons-injectivity),
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:191:  -- exactly as in `Automaton/Unambiguous`.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:199:    -- the recursive call arrives as `k`: a `where` binding would hide the
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:200:    -- structural descent, so `cancel` applies itself in its clause body
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:226:  -- The greedy answer, with maximality typed.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:228:  -- a match: a run ending at an *accepting* state
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:232:  -- ...together with a refutation of every nonempty continuation of the
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:233:  -- language *of the state it ended in*
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:244:    -- a state rejecting ε has no empty run
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:251:    -- an accepting state's empty run, as a `Match` at itself
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:266:  -- A dead successor is refuted without reading the table.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:267:  --
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:268:  -- `dead-empty` says `Trace true q` is uninhabited, which is exactly the
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:269:  -- hypothesis `unmatched` wants -- so a dead `δ q c` answers with no `π`
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:270:  -- on the tail at all.  That is the early exit: forcing the tail's cell
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:271:  -- is what makes one token cost the whole remaining input.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:285:      -- extending the match is one `STEP`: O(1), and no derivative
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:311:      -- dead: the refutation comes from the certificate, and the tail is
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:312:      -- discarded rather than projected.  Alive: exactly the old path.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:368:    -- ...and the bridge is an iso.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:369:    --
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:370:    -- `Automaton/Unambiguous`'s induction, with the acceptance equation
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:371:    -- replaced by the end-state equation.  Together with `endState` --
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:372:    -- which says the `⊕[ q' ]` of `BridgeTy` is a singleton -- it makes
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:373:    -- `BridgeTy b q` a proposition, and two propositions with maps both
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:374:    -- ways are isomorphic.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:396:      -- the recursive call stands in the clause body, as in `Unambiguous`
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:441:    -- the end state is determined, the acceptance equation is a
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:442:    -- proposition, and the trace is unambiguous
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:469:  -- Maximality, cashed in.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:470:  --
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:471:  -- The cheap state-indexed witness maps into the self-certifying
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:472:  -- word-indexed `Greedy` of `Greedy/Base`: the match is over some `u`,
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:473:  -- and *every* nonempty extension of that same `u` is refuted -- which is
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:474:  -- what "longest" means.  `cancel` is the whole content: it turns a run
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:475:  -- of `q` over `u ++ z` into a run of `q'` over `z`, where the refutation
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:476:  -- already lives.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:496:  -- ...and therefore the greedy branch of a `Run` is a genuine longest
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:497:  -- match: no strictly longer prefix of the input is accepted from `q`.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:501:  -- the same fact spelled out.  `u` is the greedy match and `z ++ r` what
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:502:  -- is left; a nonempty `z` with `u ++ z` accepted from `q` is absurd, so
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:503:  -- `u` is the *longest* accepted prefix and not merely an accepted one.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:505:    → TraceTo q q' u                        -- the match, ending at `q'`
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/GreedyMax.agda:506:    → ¬Ty ((L q' & char⁺) ⊗ ⊤Ty) (z ++ r)   -- ...and its refutation

src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/TokenStream.agda:308:    -- `Guarded▷` would recompute the value at every suffix it is read at.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/TokenStream.agda:312:      -- Maximal munch is unique, and that is the whole refutation: the
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/TokenStream.agda:313:      -- match found here and any match a rival parse begins with are

src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/TokenStream.agda:394:      -- payment put strictly below

src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/TokenStream.agda:435:      -- initial state, and no scan is restarted

src/Theory/Type/Guarded/Justification.agda:1:-- Semantic justification for using guarded recursion
src/Theory/Type/Guarded/Justification.agda:2:-- TODO how much of this is actually used?
src/Theory/Type/Guarded/Justification.agda:3:{-# OPTIONS --lossy-unification -WnoUnsupportedIndexedMatch #-}
src/Theory/Type/Guarded/Justification.agda:88:      -- memoising changes the cost, not the value
src/Theory/Type/Guarded/Justification.agda:93:  -- ... and the order from a measure.  This is the *only* place a consumer's
src/Theory/Type/Guarded/Justification.agda:94:  -- size argument has to go.
src/Theory/Type/Guarded/Justification.agda:104:  -- The step vocabulary of the lexicographic guard, re-exported so that a
src/Theory/Type/Guarded/Justification.agda:105:  -- consumer names it without naming an order.
src/Theory/Type/Guarded/Justification.agda:114:  -- Löb along the lexicographic (input size, index rank) order: a step either
src/Theory/Type/Guarded/Justification.agda:115:  -- shrinks the input or leaves its size alone and drops the rank.
src/Theory/Type/Guarded/Justification.agda:124:  -- The same vocabulary against an order `W` on inputs rather than their
src/Theory/Type/Guarded/Justification.agda:125:  -- size: a step either moves the input down in `W` -- takes a proper
src/Theory/Type/Guarded/Justification.agda:126:  -- suffix -- or leaves it alone and drops the rank.
src/Theory/Type/Guarded/Justification.agda:147:  -- Guardedness of a code: every recursive leaf sits strictly below the root.
src/Theory/Type/Guarded/Justification.agda:175:    -- when the recursive call does not look at its guard, `mapG` *is* `map`
src/Theory/Type/Guarded/Justification.agda:194:    -- coalgebra + algebra + guardedness, by löb
src/Theory/Type/Guarded/Justification.agda:201:      -- the löb family is `A ⇒ B` pointwise, which is where `isSetB` is spent
src/Theory/Type/Guarded/Justification.agda:237:  -- Löb by structural recursion on a fuel.
src/Theory/Type/Guarded/Justification.agda:238:  --
src/Theory/Type/Guarded/Justification.agda:239:  -- `löbByMeasure` builds `▷` from the direct category's downsets, so
src/Theory/Type/Guarded/Justification.agda:240:  -- eliminating one applies a presheaf hom whose value comes from a match on
src/Theory/Type/Guarded/Justification.agda:241:  -- an `Acc`, and the fixed point unfolds only up to a path.  Here `▷ x` *is*
src/Theory/Type/Guarded/Justification.agda:242:  -- the type of eliminations along `R`, `app` is application, and the fixed
src/Theory/Type/Guarded/Justification.agda:243:  -- point recurses on an ordinary numeral.  The bound proofs are carried and
src/Theory/Type/Guarded/Justification.agda:244:  -- never matched, so nothing blocks: `löb φ x m tt` reduces to `φ`'s body on

src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Decidable/STLC.agda:1693:-- the context is deterministic...
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Decidable/STLC.agda:1701:-- ...and so, every binder being annotated, is the typing

src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Greedy/Examples.agda:47:-- 1. A greedy match: `a+` against "aaab".
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Greedy/Examples.agda:48:--

src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Greedy/Examples.agda:51:-- and "b" refutes every nonempty way of doing so.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Greedy/Examples.agda:56:-- what is left of `a+` once it has matched: the residual
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Greedy/Examples.agda:63:-- the match itself, decided rather than asserted
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Greedy/Examples.agda:67:-- ...and the refutation: "b" starts no continuation.  `noExt-step`
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Greedy/Examples.agda:68:-- reduces "no nonempty extension" to "no derivative after this letter",
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Greedy/Examples.agda:69:-- which is where the precision of `literal` does the work.

src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Greedy/Examples.agda:103:--
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Greedy/Examples.agda:104:-- `extendAt` moves a letter out of the input and into the match.  `A` is
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Greedy/Examples.agda:105:-- ⊗-shaped on purpose: its witness carries a splitting, and it is that
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Greedy/Examples.agda:106:-- splitting the substitution inside `lit⊗Dl` has to carry.  With a
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Greedy/Examples.agda:107:-- `⊤Ty` there is nothing to carry and the step is trivially cheap,

src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/Lexicon.agda:251:  -- state -- automaton data -- and not something read off a parse.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/Lexicon.agda:252:  --
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/Lexicon.agda:253:  -- `Tok` spans the whole input: the `⊗` of `GreedyMax` splits it into
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/Lexicon.agda:254:  -- the lexeme and the (refuted-as-extendable) remainder.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/Lexicon.agda:259:  -- The readout, as a `⊢`-term.  `⊗⊕ᴰ-distL` pulls the acceptance
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/Lexicon.agda:260:  -- equation out of the left factor, `winner!` turns it into the winning
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/Lexicon.agda:261:  -- rule, and the match is put back exactly as it came.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/Lexicon.agda:266:    -- `find` runs on the *state*, which is the `⊕ᴰ` tag the scan already
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/Lexicon.agda:267:    -- produced; the match's own acceptance equation is reached only in
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/Lexicon.agda:268:    -- the branch where the state rejects, which the equation refutes.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/Lexicon.agda:269:    -- Reaching for it in the live branch instead would force the `Match`
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/Lexicon.agda:270:    -- pair at every one of the match's characters, and the readout would
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/Lexicon.agda:271:    -- be quadratic.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/Lexicon.agda:284:  -- The display boundary.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/Lexicon.agda:285:  --
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/Lexicon.agda:286:  -- `semact-text` recovers the characters under *any* grammar without
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/Lexicon.agda:287:  -- looking at its parse: `⊤Ty-intro` forgets it, `readChars` reads the
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/Lexicon.agda:288:  -- word back as a `char *`, and `semact-string` is the structural fold
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/Lexicon.agda:289:  -- on that star.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/Lexicon.agda:294:  -- The winning rule, read at the display boundary.  `Wins q'` already
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/Lexicon.agda:295:  -- names it, but projecting it out of the `⊕ᴰ` tag re-evaluates the
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/Lexicon.agda:296:  -- state; `winner` recomputes it directly from the state instead, and
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/Lexicon.agda:297:  -- the certificate serves the branch it rules out.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/Lexicon.agda:301:  -- rule index, lexeme, remainder
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Automaton/Lexicon.agda:312:  -- ...observed at a word.  This is the *only* place a `Maybe` appears,

src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Grammars/PolyGrammar.agda:198:-- Q ::= / m K | K.  The left-factoring is the whole content of this type:
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Grammars/PolyGrammar.agda:199:-- after a numeral comes a denominator, or anything a `K` admits.

src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Grammars/PolyGrammar.agda:223:-- this is the grammar.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Grammars/PolyGrammar.agda:254:  -- ...and `Q`'s, which are `K`'s but for the denominator
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Grammars/PolyGrammar.agda:315:-- The one unrolling, as grammars built from the combinators.  Every code
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Grammars/PolyGrammar.agda:316:-- leaf is a `Lift`, and `⊗e` is the arity-indexed `⊗ᵘ`; `⟦⊗e⟧` and
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Grammars/PolyGrammar.agda:317:-- `lowerTy` are what move across, one symbol at a time.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Grammars/PolyGrammar.agda:403:-- ...and the three grammars carrying their h-level, which is what a parser
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Grammars/PolyGrammar.agda:404:-- is indexed by.  From here on it is `seq`, `tok`, `_<|>_`, `nil`, `call`
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Grammars/PolyGrammar.agda:405:-- and `fix`, as in `Dyck`: three mutually recursive parsers, one per
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Grammars/PolyGrammar.agda:406:-- nonterminal, tied by Löb at their `&ᴰ`.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Grammars/PolyGrammar.agda:417:-- The route tables.  These are the whole content of "the grammar is
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Grammars/PolyGrammar.agda:418:-- LL(1)", and they are answer-free: a table is a table at every answer.
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Grammars/PolyGrammar.agda:419:--
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Grammars/PolyGrammar.agda:420:-- `E` routes on one token outright, because every `ETag` is *determined*
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Grammars/PolyGrammar.agda:421:-- by the token that starts its production -- `atom v` by `var v`, `num n`
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Grammars/PolyGrammar.agda:422:-- by `nat n`.  `K` and `Q` do not: `pow n` and `den m` carry a numeral
src/Theory/Instances/Monoid/Combinator/Grammars/PolyGrammar.agda:423:-- from the *second* token, so no `M₁ → Maybe KTag` exists.  The repair is

src/Theory/Instances/Bags/Order.agda:110:-- ⊓ -- by uniqueness of homomorphisms, with no induction over bags.
